## Post 04 · A fear of losing the wrong customer

**Type:** Entertain · **Why it works:** Explains the psychology behind a common mistake.

### TikTok · 13:45 · Talking head, 20s

**Hook (0-3s)**
> Broad offers are a fear of losing the wrong customer. So you lose the right one.

**On-screen beats**
- Keep everyone
- Sound like no one
- The right customer cannot see themselves
- They leave first

**Voiceover script**
> Broad offers are a fear of losing the wrong customer, and the result is you lose the right one. When you try to be for everybody, nobody can see themselves in the sentence, and the person with a specific problem and money to solve it is the first to leave, because they need to be understood more than they need a bargain.

**Caption**
> Trying to keep everyone loses the person who would have paid.

**Hashtags:** #LagosBusiness #Easywurld #SmallBusinessNigeria #HotTake

**Visual:** Talking head to camera, one jump cut on the disagreement.

### Instagram · 14:15 · 3-slide carousel

**Slide copy**
1. Fear: lose someone
2. Result: sound like no one
3. The paying customer leaves first

**Caption**
> Being understood is worth more than being available.

**Hashtags:** #LagosBusiness #Easywurld #SmallBusinessNigeria #HotTake

**Visual:** Talking head to camera, one jump cut on the disagreement.

### Facebook · 13:30 · Text post

> Broad offers are usually a fear of losing the wrong customer. The result is that you lose the right one.
> 
> When nobody can see themselves in your sentence, the person with a specific problem and money to solve it leaves first — because they need to feel understood more than they need a bargain.
> 
> Who are you actually for?

## Post 10 · The "do everything" rebrand

That is the only kind of sales story I trust.

**Caption**

## Post 01 · "Trusted by many"

**Type:** Entertain · **Why it works:** A sentence everyone has written and nobody believes.

### TikTok · 07:00 · Screen recording, 23s

**Hook (0-3s)**
> This site says trusted by many and shows nothing at all.

**On-screen beats**
- Trusted by many
- Who
- How many
- Show one

**Voiceover script**
> This site says trusted by many, which is a sentence that has never persuaded anybody, because it is the same sentence a business with no customers would write. Vague trust is not weak trust, it is no trust, and it is doing damage, because it tells the reader you have nothing to show and decided to describe it instead.

**Caption**
> "Trusted by many" is what a business with nothing to show writes.

**Hashtags:** #LagosBusiness #WebsiteDesign #SmallBusinessNigeria #Easywurld

2. By who?
3. Show one

**Hashtags:** #LagosBusiness #WebsiteDesign #SmallBusinessNigeria #Easywurld

**Visual:** Screen recording of the page, cream frame, one terracotta arrow on the problem.

### Facebook · 08:00 · Text post

> A site I read this week says 'trusted by many' and shows nothing at all.
> 
> That is the sentence a business with no customers would write.
> 
> Vague trust is not weak trust. It is no trust — and it tells the reader you had nothing to show, so you described it instead.

> What could you show this week?

## Post 03 · Show the work, not the praise

**Type:** Teach · **Why it works:** A structural alternative to the testimonial section.

### TikTok · 11:30 · Kinetic type, 22s

**Hook (0-3s)**
> A before and after beats a quote. Show the work, not the praise.

**On-screen beats**
- Before
- After
- Same angle
- No adjectives

**Voiceover script**
> A before and after beats a quote, because a quote is a claim about you and a before and after is evidence. Same angle, same light, same corner of the room, and no adjectives anywhere. If you have not got a before photo, take one on the next job, before you touch anything, and you will have a year of proof by December.

**Caption**
> Same angle, same light, no adjectives. That is a case study.

**Hashtags:** #SmallBusinessTips #LagosSME #Easywurld #MarketingTips

**Visual:** Kinetic type on cream, one line at a time, mark bottom-right.

### Instagram · 12:30 · 4-slide carousel

**Slide copy**
1. Before
2. After
3. Same angle
4. No adjectives

**Caption**
> Start photographing the before. Most people only photograph the after.

**Hashtags:** #SmallBusinessTips #LagosSME #Easywurld #MarketingTips

**Visual:** Kinetic type on cream, one line at a time, mark bottom-right.

### Facebook · 12:00 · Text post

> A before and after beats a quote.
> 
> A quote is a claim about you. A before and after is evidence, and it needs no adjectives at all — same angle, same light, same corner of the room.
> 
> If you have never taken a before photo, take one on the next job, before you touch anything.
> 
> By December you will have a year of proof.

## Post 10 · Asked to invent a testimonial

**Type:** Entertain · **Why it works:** Naming the request makes the refusal credible.

### TikTok · 20:45 · Talking head, 31s

**Hook (0-3s)**
> Someone asked us to write a testimonial for them to publish. We said no.

**On-screen beats**
- It would take ten minutes
- It would never be true
- And they would have to defend it
- Forever

# How to publish this from your phone

**One file.** `EASYWURLD_POST_COMMANDER.html` is a single file, works with no internet, and
saves your tick marks on the phone. Add it to your home screen and it behaves like an app.

**Free scheduling, no new apps.**

| Platform | How to schedule | Cost |
|---|---|---|
| Instagram + Facebook | Meta Business Suite, timezone Africa/Lagos | Free |
| TikTok | Schedule option inside the app for business accounts | Free |
| LinkedIn | The schedule button in the post composer | Free |

**Set the whole month in one sitting.** Every post above is dated, so the first Sunday of the
month you can schedule all thirty days at once. After that it is 20 minutes a week. Leave the
two slots a week that need a real human reply open, and do not schedule a post you have not
read out loud once.

**Where to schedule from.** `content_calendar.csv` is the same plan as a Google Sheet. Sort by
date, and every row carries its time, its format and the full copy, so you never have to
remember which post goes where.

**Storage.** Keep images and videos in Google Drive, not on the phone. Open them from Drive
when you need them. Text is cheap, video is not.

**Reply first, post second.** A reply to a real customer beats one more post. If a slot
arrives and a customer is waiting, answer the customer and let the post wait.
